# pcm-only: files 26701–27000 of 57745

[Catalogue index](index.md). [Method and limits](../hvsc-rom-requirements.md).

K-only removes BASIC; B-only removes KERNAL. All rows include duration, evidence class, reference RMS/write count, and the three removal outcomes. `derived` marks proven zero-read equivalence, not a separate render.

>          didn't like it much and thus never considered using it." (JCH)
>
> COMMENT: "This is the only tune I did in Future Composer, made at the Dexion
>          copy-party in 1988, the same party where I was told by Laxity to keep
>          my hands off his player. The Future Composer was a hack editor on
>          Charles Deenen's player and it arrived at this party for the first
>          time. I tried it out once - and never touched it again. 'Typhus' was
>          the name of the german guy who also ordered the 'Sharks' music in the
>          old player." (JCH)

>          where other versions required $F8 to achieve the same effect." (JCH)
